# Deck optimizer results (2026-10-25-pro3-maptrue)

Generated by `tools/opt/deckopt.js` (packages OPT, OPT2). Your level: 1. Fair mirror: both sides at your level, your bramble, CPU skill 1, seats swapped; "road" = the fight as the game builds it. Score = 0.75 mirror + 0.25 road unless the settings say otherwise. Elo gap = 400 log10(p / (1 - p)) of the optimum's head-to-head score against that deck (+200 = the optimum wins 76%, +400 = 91%, +600 = 97%). "Owned" = a Base card, or a card obtainable before this map (HUB3 progression plan). "Candidate" = a phase-2 card (tools/opt/phase2-rules.json).
